 6507:         committed = {
 6508:             item.get("commit")
 6509:             for item in state.get("completed_commits", [])
 6510:             if isinstance(item, dict)
 6511:         }
 6512:         return [
 6513:             criterion["id"]
 6514:             for criterion in master_plan["global_criteria"]
 6515:             if not isinstance(evidence.get(criterion["id"]), dict)
 6516:             or evidence[criterion["id"]].get("status") != "PASS"
 6517:             or evidence[criterion["id"]].get("commit") not in committed
 6518:             or not isinstance(evidence[criterion["id"]].get("task_scope"), list)
 6519:             or not evidence[criterion["id"]].get("task_scope")
 6520:             or not isinstance(evidence[criterion["id"]].get("task_id"), str)
 6521:             or not isinstance(evidence[criterion["id"]].get("base_commit"), str)
 6522:             or not isinstance(evidence[criterion["id"]].get("candidate_id"), str)
 6523:             or not evidence[criterion["id"]].get("review_ref")
 6524:             or not evidence[criterion["id"]].get("verification_ref")
 6525:         ]
 6526: 
 6527:     @staticmethod
 6528:     def _durable_replan_attempt_count(run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
 6529:         values: list[int] = []
 6530:         for path in run_dir.glob("replan_reason_*.json"):
 6531:             match = re.fullmatch(r"replan_reason_([0-9]+)\.json", path.name)
 6532:             if match:
 6533:                 values.append(int(match.group(1)))
 6534:         reason_ref = state.get("replan_reason_ref")
 6535:         if isinstance(reason_ref, str):
 6536:             match = re.fullmatch(r"replan_reason_([0-9]+)\.json", reason_ref)
 6537:             if match:
 6538:                 values.append(int(match.group(1)))
 6539:         return max(values, default=0)
 6540: 
 6541:     @staticmethod
 6542:     def _durable_replan_dispatch_count(run_dir: Path) -> int:
 6543:         values = []
 6544:         for path in run_dir.glob("replan_dispatch_*.json"):
 6545:             match = re.fullmatch(r"replan_dispatch_([0-9]+)\.json", path.name)
 6546:             if match:
 6547:                 values.append(int(match.group(1)))
 6548:         return max(values, default=0)
 6549: 
 6550:     @staticmethod
 6551:     def _review_replan_reason(
 6552:         review_ref: str, review: dict[str, Any], state: dict[str, Any]
 6553:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 6554:         original = validate_replan_reason(review.get("replan_reason"))
 6555:         evidence = dict(original["evidence"])
 6556:         evidence["source_artifact_ref"] = evidence.get("artifact_ref")
 6557:         evidence["artifact_ref"] = review_ref
 6558:         evidence["review_ref"] = review_ref
 6559:         evidence["producer"] = "Sol Reviewer"
 6560:         evidence["run_id"] = state.get("run_id")
 6561:         evidence["master_plan_ref"] = state.get("master_plan_ref")
 6562:         evidence["request_ref"] = state.get("request_ref")
 6563:         evidence["task_id"] = state.get("current_task_id")
 6564:         evidence["base_commit"] = review.get("base_commit")
 6565:         evidence["candidate_id"] = review.get("candidate_id")
 6566:         evidence["candidate_ref"] = state.get("candidate_ref")
 6567:         evidence["verification_ref"] = state.get("verification_ref")
 6568:         evidence["verification"] = review.get("verification")
 6569:         match = re.fullmatch(r"sol_review_([0-9]+)\.json", review_ref)
 6570:         evidence["sequence"] = int(match.group(1)) if match else None
 6571:         return {"code": original["code"], "evidence": evidence}
 6572: 
 6573:     def _validate_replan_evidence(
 6574:         self,
 6575:         run_dir: Path,
 6576:         reason: dict[str, Any],
 6577:         state: dict[str, Any],
 6578:         *,
 6579:         historical: bool = False,
 6580:     ) -> str:
 6581:         evidence = reason["evidence"]
 6582:         artifact_ref = evidence.get("artifact_ref")
 6583:         if not isinstance(artifact_ref, str) or not re.fullmatch(
 6584:             r"(?:request_change_[0-9a-f]{32}|sol_review_[0-9]+|replan_evidence_[0-9]+)\.json",
 6585:             artifact_ref,
 6586:         ):
 6587:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence is not a bounded host artifact", "blocked")
 6588:         artifact = (run_dir / artifact_ref).resolve()
 6589:         try:
 6590:             artifact.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 6591:         except ValueError as exc:
 6592:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence reference escapes the run directory", "blocked") from exc
 6593:         if not artifact.is_file():
 6594:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence artifact is missing", "blocked")
 6595:         value = read_json(artifact)
 6596:         code = reason["code"]
 6597:         kind = REPLAN_REASON_KINDS[code]
 6598: 
 6599:         if code == "USER_OBJECTIVE_OR_SCOPE_CHANGE":
 6600:             config = state.get("config")
 6601:             valid = (
 6602:                 artifact_ref == state.get("request_ref")
 6603:                 and isinstance(config, dict)
 6604:                 and isinstance(value, dict)
 6605:                 and set(value) == {"request", "requested_files", "reason"}
 6606:                 and value.get("request") == config.get("request")
 6607:                 and value.get("requested_files") == config.get("requested_files")
 6608:                 and isinstance(value.get("requested_files"), list)
 6609:                 and isinstance(value.get("reason"), dict)
 6610:                 and value["reason"].get("code") == code
 6611:                 and value["reason"].get("evidence", {}).get("artifact_ref") == artifact_ref
 6612:                 and value["reason"].get("evidence", {}).get("kind") == kind
 6613:                 and value["reason"].get("evidence", {}).get("verified") is True
 6614:             )
 6615:             if not valid:
 6616:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence is not the current immutable user request artifact", "blocked")
 6617:         elif code in {
 6618:             "ASSUMPTION_DISPROVED",
 6619:             "REQUIRED_GAP_UNCOVERED",
 6620:             "ARCHITECTURE_DEPENDENCY_CONFLICT",
 6621:         } and re.fullmatch(r"sol_review_[0-9]+\.json", artifact_ref):
 6622:             identity_fields = (
 6623:                 "run_id",
 6624:                 "master_plan_ref",
 6625:                 "request_ref",
 6626:                 "task_id",
 6627:                 "base_commit",
 6628:                 "candidate_id",
 6629:                 "candidate_ref",
 6630:                 "review_ref",
 6631:                 "verification_ref",
 6632:                 "verification",
 6633:                 "sequence",
 6634:             )
 6635:             if any(field not in evidence for field in identity_fields):
 6636:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence Sol reason lacks complete identity", "blocked")
 6637:             candidate_ref = evidence["candidate_ref"]
 6638:             if not re.fullmatch(r"candidate_[0-9]+\.json", candidate_ref):
 6639:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence has no bounded candidate lineage", "blocked")
 6640:             candidate_path = (run_dir / candidate_ref).resolve()
 6641:             if not candidate_path.is_file():
 6642:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence candidate artifact is missing", "blocked")
 6643:             candidate = read_json(candidate_path)
 6644:             plan_ref = evidence["master_plan_ref"]
 6645:             plan_path = (run_dir / plan_ref).resolve() if isinstance(plan_ref, str) else None
 6646:             config = state.get("config")
 6647:             if plan_path is None or not re.fullmatch(r"plans/[0-9a-f]{64}\.json", plan_ref.replace("\\", "/")):
 6648:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence has no bounded master plan lineage", "blocked")
 6649:             if not plan_path.is_file():
 6650:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence master plan artifact is missing", "blocked")
 6651:             plan = read_json(plan_path)
 6652:             canonical_plan = json.dumps(plan, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
 6653:             if hashlib.sha256(canonical_plan.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() != plan_ref.split("/")[-1][:-5]:
 6654:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence master plan lineage is stale", "blocked")
 6655:             if not isinstance(config, dict) or not isinstance(config.get("requested_files"), list):
 6656:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence has no current request scope", "blocked")
 6657:             plan = validate_master_plan(plan, None)
 6658:             task_id = evidence["task_id"]
 6659:             task = next((item for item in master_plan_tasks(plan) if item["id"] == task_id), None)
 6660:             if task is None:
 6661:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence task identity is stale", "blocked")
 6662:             if (
 6663:                 not isinstance(candidate, dict)
 6664:                 or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", str(candidate.get("sha256")))
 6665:                 or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", str(candidate.get("base_commit")))
 6666:                 or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", str(candidate.get("candidate_id")))
 6667:                 or candidate.get("base_commit") != evidence.get("base_commit")
 6668:                 or candidate.get("candidate_id") != evidence.get("candidate_id")
 6669:             ):
 6670:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence candidate lineage is invalid", "blocked")
 6671:             review = value
 6672:             required_review_keys = {
 6673:                 "decision",
 6674:                 "reviewed_diff_sha256",
 6675:                 "base_commit",
 6676:                 "candidate_id",
 6677:                 "criteria",
 6678:                 "findings",
 6679:                 "required_changes",
 6680:                 "route",
 6681:                 "verification",
 6682:                 "replan_reason",
 6683:             }
 6684:             if not isinstance(review, dict) or set(review) != required_review_keys:
 6685:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence Sol review schema is invalid", "blocked")
 6686:             review_reason = validate_replan_reason(review["replan_reason"])
 6687:             trigger_evidence = review_reason["evidence"]
 6688:             common_identity = (
 6689:                 "artifact_ref",
 6690:                 "kind",
 6691:                 "verified",
 6692:                 "producer",
 6693:                 "run_id",
 6694:                 "master_plan_ref",
 6695:                 "request_ref",
 6696:                 "task_id",
 6697:                 "base_commit",
 6698:                 "candidate_id",
 6699:                 "candidate_ref",
 6700:                 "verification_ref",
 6701:                 "verification",
 6702:                 "sequence",
 6703:             )
 6704:             if any(
 6705:                 trigger_evidence.get(field) != evidence.get(field)
 6706:                 for field in common_identity
 6707:             ):
 6708:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence Sol reason identity is inconsistent", "blocked")
 6709:             if code == "ASSUMPTION_DISPROVED":
 6710:                 assumption_ids = {
 6711:                     item["id"] for item in plan.get("assumptions", []) if isinstance(item, dict)
 6712:                 }
 6713:                 trigger_valid = (
 6714:                     isinstance(trigger_evidence.get("assumption_id"), str)
 6715:                     and trigger_evidence["assumption_id"] in assumption_ids
 6716:                 )
 6717:             elif code == "REQUIRED_GAP_UNCOVERED":
 6718:                 criterion_ids = {
 6719:                     item["id"]
 6720:                     for item in plan.get("global_criteria", [])
 6721:                     if isinstance(item, dict)
 6722:                 }
 6723:                 criterion_ids.update(item["id"] for item in task.get("acceptance_criteria", []))
 6724:                 trigger_valid = (
 6725:                     isinstance(trigger_evidence.get("criterion_id"), str)
 6726:                     and trigger_evidence["criterion_id"] in criterion_ids
 6727:                 )
 6728:             else:
 6729:                 valid_edges = {
 6730:                     f"{dependency}->{item['id']}"
 6731:                     for item in master_plan_tasks(plan)
 6732:                     for dependency in item.get("depends_on", [])
 6733:                 }
 6734:                 trigger_valid = trigger_evidence.get("dependency") in valid_edges
 6735:             verification_ref = evidence["verification_ref"]
 6736:             if not isinstance(verification_ref, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"test_[0-9]+\.json", verification_ref):
 6737:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence has no bounded verification lineage", "blocked")
 6738:             verification_contract = self._verification_contract(run_dir, verification_ref)
 6739:             if (
 6740:                 review_reason["code"] != code
 6741:                 or review_reason["evidence"].get("kind") != kind
 6742:                 or review_reason["evidence"].get("verified") is not True
 6743:                 or not trigger_valid
 6744:                 or review.get("base_commit") != candidate["base_commit"]
 6745:                 or review.get("candidate_id") != candidate["candidate_id"]
 6746:                 or review.get("reviewed_diff_sha256") != candidate["sha256"]
 6747:                 or evidence["run_id"] != state.get("run_id")
 6748:                 or evidence["master_plan_ref"] != plan_ref
 6749:                 or (not historical and evidence["request_ref"] != state.get("request_ref"))
 6750:                 or evidence["task_id"] != task_id
 6751:                 or evidence["base_commit"] != candidate["base_commit"]
 6752:                 or evidence["candidate_id"] != candidate["candidate_id"]
 6753:                 or evidence["candidate_ref"] != candidate_ref
 6754:                 or evidence["review_ref"] != artifact_ref
 6755:                 or evidence["sequence"] != int(re.fullmatch(r"sol_review_([0-9]+)\.json", artifact_ref).group(1))
 6756:                 or not isinstance(review.get("verification"), dict)
 6757:                 or review["verification"].get("artifact_ref") != verification_ref
 6758:                 or evidence["verification"] != review.get("verification")
 6759:                 or review.get("verification") != verification_contract
 6760:             ):
 6761:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence Sol review is not bound to the current candidate", "blocked")
 6762:             validate_review(
 6763:                 review,
 6764:                 candidate["sha256"],
 6765:                 candidate["base_commit"],
 6766:                 candidate["candidate_id"],
 6767:                 self._task_review_criteria_ids(self._task_execution_plan(plan, task)),
 6768:                 verification_contract,
 6769:             )
 6770:         elif code in {"ARCHITECTURE_DEPENDENCY_CONFLICT", "PLAN_EXHAUSTED_OBJECTIVE_INCOMPLETE"} and re.fullmatch(
 6771:             r"replan_evidence_[0-9]+\.json", artifact_ref
 6772:         ):
 6773:             expected_plan_ref = state.get("master_plan_ref")
 6774:             plan_path = (run_dir / expected_plan_ref).resolve() if isinstance(expected_plan_ref, str) else None
 6775:             if plan_path is None or not plan_path.is_file():
 6776:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence has no current master plan", "blocked")
 6777:             plan = read_json(plan_path)
 6778:             expected_completed = sorted(
 6779:                 item.get("task_id")
 6780:                 for item in state.get("completed_commits", [])
 6781:                 if isinstance(item, dict) and isinstance(item.get("task_id"), str)
 6782:             )
 6783:             expected_gaps = self._remaining_master_plan_gaps(plan, state)
 6784:             expected_pending = sorted(
 6785:                 task["id"]
 6786:                 for task in master_plan_tasks(plan)
 6787:                 if task["id"] not in set(expected_completed)
 6788:             )
 6789:             expected_sequence = int(re.fullmatch(r"replan_evidence_([0-9]+)\.json", artifact_ref).group(1))
 6790:             required_keys = {
 6791:                 "artifact_type",
 6792:                 "replan_code",
 6793:                 "kind",
 6794:                 "verified",
 6795:                 "producer",
 6796:                 "run_id",
 6797:                 "master_plan_ref",
 6798:                 "request_ref",
 6799:                 "base_commit",
 6800:                 "task_id",
 6801:                 "candidate_id",
 6802:                 "sequence",
 6803:                 "remaining_gaps",
 6804:                 "completed_task_ids",
 6805:                 "blocked_task_ids",
 6806:             }
 6807:             valid = (
 6808:                 isinstance(value, dict)
 6809:                 and set(value) == required_keys
 6810:                 and value.get("artifact_type") == "hermes_replan_evidence_v1"
 6811:                 and value.get("replan_code") == code
 6812:                 and value.get("kind") == kind
 6813:                 and value.get("verified") is True
 6814:                 and value.get("producer") == "Hermes"
 6815:                 and value.get("run_id") == state.get("run_id")
 6816:                 and value.get("master_plan_ref") == expected_plan_ref
 6817:                 and value.get("request_ref") == state.get("request_ref")
 6818:                 and value.get("base_commit") == state.get("base_commit")
 6819:                 and value.get("task_id") == state.get("current_task_id")
 6820:                 and value.get("candidate_id") == state.get("candidate_id")
 6821:                 and value.get("sequence") == expected_sequence
 6822:                 and value.get("completed_task_ids") == expected_completed
 6823:                 and value.get("remaining_gaps") == expected_gaps
 6824:                 and value.get("blocked_task_ids") == expected_pending
 6825:             )
 6826:             if code == "PLAN_EXHAUSTED_OBJECTIVE_INCOMPLETE":
 6827:                 valid = valid and bool(expected_gaps) and not expected_pending
 6828:             else:
 6829:                 valid = valid and bool(expected_pending)
 6830:             if not valid:
 6831:                 raise FactoryError("replan evidence is not the current Hermes planning artifact", "blocked")
 6832:         else:
 6833:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence is not code-appropriate verified evidence", "blocked")
 6834: 
 6835:         digest = hashlib.sha256(artifact.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
 6836:         recorded = evidence.get("artifact_sha256")
 6837:         if recorded is not None and recorded != digest:
 6838:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence artifact changed after reservation", "blocked")
 6839:         return digest
 6840: 
 6841:     def _validate_replan_history(self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
 6842:         reason_paths = sorted(
 6843:             (
 6844:                 path
 6845:                 for path in run_dir.glob("replan_reason_*.json")
 6846:                 if re.fullmatch(r"replan_reason_[0-9]+\.json", path.name)
 6847:             ),
 6848:             key=lambda path: int(re.search(r"([0-9]+)", path.stem).group(1)),
 6849:         )
 6850:         if not reason_paths:
 6851:             if state.get("replan_reason_ref") is not None:
 6852:                 raise FactoryError("replan reason history is missing its referenced artifact", "blocked")
 6853:             return
 6854:         numbers = [int(re.search(r"([0-9]+)", path.stem).group(1)) for path in reason_paths]
 6855:         if numbers != list(range(1, len(numbers) + 1)):
 6856:             raise FactoryError("replan reason history is not contiguous", "blocked")
 6857:         if state.get("replan_reason_ref") != reason_paths[-1].name:
 6858:             raise FactoryError("replan reason history has an invalid current reference", "blocked")
 6859:         for reason_path in reason_paths:
 6860:             reason = validate_replan_reason(read_json(reason_path))
 6861:             evidence = reason["evidence"]
 6862:             artifact_ref = evidence.get("artifact_ref")
 6863:             if not isinstance(artifact_ref, str):
 6864:                 raise FactoryError("replan reason history has an invalid evidence reference", "blocked")
 6865:             artifact = (run_dir / artifact_ref).resolve()
 6866:             try:
 6867:                 artifact.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 6868:             except ValueError as exc:
 6869:                 raise FactoryError("replan reason history evidence escapes the run", "blocked") from exc
 6870:             recorded_hash = evidence.get("artifact_sha256")
 6871:             if not artifact.is_file() or not isinstance(recorded_hash, str):
 6872:                 raise FactoryError("replan reason history evidence is missing", "blocked")
 6873:             if hashlib.sha256(artifact.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != recorded_hash:
 6874:                 raise FactoryError("replan reason history evidence integrity is stale", "blocked")
 6875:             if artifact_ref.startswith("sol_review_"):
 6876:                 self._validate_replan_evidence(run_dir, reason, state, historical=True)
 6877:                 continue
 6878:             if artifact_ref.startswith("request_change_"):
 6879:                 value = read_json(artifact)
 6880:                 nested = value.get("reason") if isinstance(value, dict) else None
 6881:                 nested_evidence = nested.get("evidence") if isinstance(nested, dict) else None
 6882:                 if (
 6883:                     not re.fullmatch(r"request_change_[0-9a-f]{32}\.json", artifact_ref)
 6884:                     or not isinstance(value, dict)
 6885:                     or set(value) != {"request", "requested_files", "reason"}
 6886:                     or not isinstance(value.get("request"), str)
 6887:                     or not isinstance(value.get("requested_files"), list)
 6888:                     or not isinstance(nested, dict)
 6889:                     or nested.get("code") != reason["code"]
 6890:                     or not isinstance(nested_evidence, dict)
 6891:                     or nested_evidence.get("artifact_ref") != artifact_ref
 6892:                     or nested_evidence.get("kind") != "user_change"
 6893:                     or nested_evidence.get("verified") is not True
 6894:                 ):
 6895:                     raise FactoryError("replan reason history user artifact is malformed", "blocked")
 6896:                 continue
 6897:             if artifact_ref.startswith("replan_evidence_"):
 6898:                 value = read_json(artifact)
 6899:                 if (
 6900:                     not isinstance(value, dict)
 6901:                     or value.get("artifact_type") != "hermes_replan_evidence_v1"
 6902:                     or value.get("replan_code") != reason["code"]
 6903:                     or value.get("kind") != REPLAN_REASON_KINDS[reason["code"]]
 6904:                     or value.get("verified") is not True
 6905:                     or value.get("producer") != "Hermes"
 6906:                     or value.get("run_id") != state.get("run_id")
 6907:                     or not isinstance(value.get("master_plan_ref"), str)
 6908:                     or not isinstance(value.get("request_ref"), str)
 6909:                     or not isinstance(value.get("base_commit"), str)
 6910:                     or not isinstance(value.get("sequence"), int)
 6911:                     or value.get("sequence") != int(re.search(r"([0-9]+)", artifact_ref).group(1))
 6912:                 ):
 6913:                     raise FactoryError("replan reason history Hermes artifact is malformed", "blocked")
 6914:                 continue
 6915:             raise FactoryError("replan reason history contains an unknown artifact", "blocked")
 6916: 
 6917:     def _block_replan_exhaustion(
 6918:         self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], *, attempts: int, reason: dict[str, Any] | None = None
 6919:     ) -> None:
 6920:         block_reason = "exceptional replan budget exhausted; run is blocked"
RANGE 6920-7150
 6920:         block_reason = "exceptional replan budget exhausted; run is blocked"
 6921:         evidence = {
 6922:             "reason": block_reason,
 6923:             "replan_attempts": attempts,
 6924:             "last_reason": reason,
 6925:             "last_reason_ref": state.get("replan_reason_ref"),
 6926:         }
 6927:         atomic_json(run_dir / "replan_blocked.json", evidence)
 6928:         self._save(
 6929:             run_dir,
 6930:             state,
 6931:             "blocked",
 6932:             block_reason=block_reason,
 6933:             error=block_reason,
 6934:             error_category="blocked",
 6935:             replan_block_evidence="replan_blocked.json",
 6936:         )
 6937:         raise FactoryError(block_reason, "blocked")
 6938: 
 6939:     def _persist_replan_reason(
 6940:         self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], reason: dict[str, Any]
 6941:     ) -> str:
 6942:         reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
 6943:         artifact_ref = reason["evidence"]["artifact_ref"]
 6944:         artifact_path = (run_dir / artifact_ref).resolve()
 6945:         try:
 6946:             artifact_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 6947:         except ValueError as exc:
 6948:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence reference escapes the run directory", "blocked") from exc
 6949:         if not artifact_path.is_file():
 6950:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence artifact is missing", "blocked")
 6951:         evidence_hash = self._validate_replan_evidence(run_dir, reason, state)
 6952:         reason["evidence"]["artifact_sha256"] = evidence_hash
 6953:         attempt = self._durable_replan_attempt_count(run_dir, state) + 1
 6954:         if self._durable_replan_dispatch_count(run_dir) >= 2:
 6955:             self._block_replan_exhaustion(
 6956:                 run_dir,
 6957:                 state,
 6958:                 attempts=self._durable_replan_dispatch_count(run_dir),
 6959:                 reason=reason,
 6960:             )
 6961:         reason_ref = f"replan_reason_{attempt}.json"
 6962:         reason_path = run_dir / reason_ref
 6963:         if reason_path.exists():
 6964:             raise FactoryError("replan reason history is ambiguous", "blocked")
 6965:         atomic_json(reason_path, reason)
 6966:         self._save(run_dir, state, "planning", replan_reason_ref=reason_ref)
 6967:         return reason_ref
 6968: 
 6969:     def request_replan(
 6970:         self,
 6971:         identifier: str,
 6972:         reason: dict[str, Any],
 6973:         *,
 6974:         objective: str | None = None,
 6975:         requested_files: list[str] | None = None,
 6976:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 6977:         """Accept only a verified structured strategic trigger for an Astra replan."""
 6978:         normalized_reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
 6979:         run_dir = self._run_dir(identifier)
 6980:         with _run_state_lock(run_dir):

 6982:             if state["phase"] in {"committed", "complete"}:

 6985:                 raise FactoryError("cannot replan with an ambiguous candidate or pending commit", "blocked")

 6987:             if normalized_reason["code"] == "USER_OBJECTIVE_OR_SCOPE_CHANGE":

 6989:                     raise FactoryError(

 6991:                         "input",

 6996:                 ensure_head_paths_not_symlinks(repo, replacement_files)

 7012:             elif objective is not None or requested_files is not None:

 7014:                     "replacement objective/scope is allowed only for USER_OBJECTIVE_OR_SCOPE_CHANGE",
 7015:                     "input",

 7020:                 identifier,
 7021:                 config.repo,

 7042:     def _reconcile_pending_commit(
 7043:         self,
 7044:         identifier: str,
 7045:         run_dir: Path,
 7046:         state: dict[str, Any],
 7047:         config: Config,
 7048:         master_plan: dict[str, Any],
 7049:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 7050:         pending = state.get("pending_commit")
 7051:         if not isinstance(pending, dict) or not isinstance(pending.get("candidate_id"), str):
 7052:             raise FactoryError("committing state has no valid pending candidate", "blocked")
 7053:         expected_candidate = pending["candidate_id"]
 7054:         task_id = state.get("current_task_id")
 7055:         if not isinstance(task_id, str):
 7056:             raise FactoryError("committing state has no current task", "blocked")
 7057:         task = next((item for item in master_plan_tasks(master_plan) if item["id"] == task_id), None)
 7058:         if task is None:
 7059:             raise FactoryError("pending commit task is not present in the master plan", "blocked")
 7060:         worktree_raw = state.get("worktree")
 7061:         if not isinstance(worktree_raw, str) or not Path(worktree_raw).is_dir():
 7062:             raise FactoryError("pending commit worktree is missing", "blocked")
 7063:         worktree = Path(worktree_raw)
 7064:         base_commit = state.get("base_commit")
 7065:         if not isinstance(base_commit, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", base_commit):
 7066:             raise FactoryError("pending commit has an invalid base commit", "blocked")
 7067:         review_ref = state.get("review_ref")
 7068:         if not isinstance(review_ref, str) or not review_ref:
 7069:             raise FactoryError("pending commit has no review reference", "blocked")
 7070:         review_path = (run_dir / review_ref).resolve()
 7071:         try:
 7072:             review_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 7073:         except ValueError as exc:
 7074:             raise FactoryError("pending review reference escapes the run directory", "blocked") from exc
 7075:         if not review_path.is_file():
 7076:             raise FactoryError("pending review artifact is missing", "blocked")

 7078:         if state.get("candidate_id") != expected_candidate:
 7079:             raise FactoryError("pending commit candidate is not bound to run state", "blocked")
 7080:         pending_base = pending.get("base_commit")
 7081:         if pending_base != base_commit:
 7082:             raise FactoryError("pending commit base does not match the approved base", "blocked")
 7083:         pending_review_hash = pending.get("reviewed_diff_sha256")
 7084:         if pending_review_hash != review.get("reviewed_diff_sha256"):
 7085:             raise FactoryError("pending commit review hash is not bound to the approval", "blocked")
 7086:         if pending.get("review_ref") != review_ref:
 7087:             raise FactoryError("pending commit review reference is not bound to the approval", "blocked")

 7094:             != state_candidate_state.get("complete_identity_sha256")

 7124: 
 7125:         head = git(worktree, "rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout.strip()

 7127:             parent = git(worktree, "rev-parse", "HEAD^").stdout.strip()

 7201:             commit_hash = git(worktree, "rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout.strip()

 7223:         state["completed_commits"] = completed_commits
 7224:         state["base_commit"] = commit_hash
 7225:         state["candidate_id"] = None
 7226:         state["pending_commit"] = None
 7227:         state["active_attempt_ref"] = None
 7228:         task_evidence = next(
 7229:             (
 7230:                 item
 7231:                 for item in (state.get("criterion_evidence") or {}).values()
 7232:                 if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("task_id") == task_id
 7233:             ),
 7234:             None,
 7235:         )
 7236:         if (
 7237:             not isinstance(task_evidence, dict)
 7238:             or not isinstance(task_evidence.get("review_sha256"), str)
 7239:             or not isinstance(task_evidence.get("verification_sha256"), str)
 7240:             or not isinstance(task_evidence.get("verification_sequence"), int)
 7241:         ):
 7242:             raise FactoryError("pending commit lacks bound verification provenance", "blocked")
 7243:         self._record_approved_criteria(
 7244:             state,
 7245:             task,
 7246:             review,
 7247:             base_commit=base_commit,
 7248:             candidate_id=expected_candidate,
 7249:             review_ref=str(state.get("review_ref")),
 7250:             verification_ref=state.get("verification_ref"),
 7251:             review_sha256=task_evidence["review_sha256"],
 7252:             verification_sha256=task_evidence["verification_sha256"],
 7253:             verification_sequence=task_evidence["verification_sequence"],
 7254:             review_criteria_ids=task_evidence.get("review_criteria_ids"),
 7255:         )

 7257:         self._bind_criteria_to_commit(state, task_id, commit_hash)

 7259:             run_dir,
 7260:             state,
 7261:             "planned",
 7262:             commit=commit_hash,
 7263:             current_task_id=task_id,
 7264:             completed_commits=completed_commits,

 7273:     def _validate_terminal_state(
 7274:         self,
 7275:         identifier: str,
 7276:         run_dir: Path,
 7277:         state: dict[str, Any],
 7278:         config: Config,
 7279:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 7280:         """Revalidate a terminal snapshot before trusting its fast path."""
 7281:         def invalid(reason: str) -> None:
 7282:             self._save(
 7283:                 run_dir,
 7284:                 state,
 7285:                 "blocked",
 7286:                 block_reason=reason,
 7287:                 error=reason,
 7288:                 error_category="blocked",
 7289:             )
 7290:             raise FactoryError(reason, "blocked")
 7291: 
 7292:         try:
 7293:             plan = self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
 7294:             replan_reason_ref = state.get("replan_reason_ref")
 7295:             if replan_reason_ref is not None:
 7296:                 if not isinstance(replan_reason_ref, str) or not re.fullmatch(
 7297:                     r"replan_reason_[0-9]+\.json", replan_reason_ref
 7298:                 ):
 7299:                     invalid("terminal state has an invalid replan reason reference")
 7300:                 replan_path = (run_dir / replan_reason_ref).resolve()
 7301:                 try:
 7302:                     replan_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 7303:                 except ValueError:
 7304:                     invalid("terminal state replan reason escapes the run")
 7305:                 if not replan_path.is_file():
 7306:                     invalid("terminal state replan reason artifact is missing")
 7307:                 persisted_reason = validate_replan_reason(read_json(replan_path))

 7309:                 if not isinstance(evidence_ref, str):
 7310:                     invalid("terminal state replan evidence reference is invalid")
 7311:                 evidence_path = (run_dir / evidence_ref).resolve()
 7312:                 try:
 7313:                     evidence_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 7314:                 except ValueError:
 7315:                     invalid("terminal state replan evidence escapes the run")

 7317:                 if (
 7318:                     not evidence_path.is_file()
 7319:                     or not isinstance(recorded_hash, str)
 7320:                     or hashlib.sha256(evidence_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != recorded_hash
 7321:                 ):
 7322:                     invalid("terminal state replan evidence integrity is stale")

 7345:                     or not isinstance(base_commit, str)
 7346:                     or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", base_commit)
 7347:                 ):

 7350:                     invalid("terminal state references a task absent from the master plan")
 7351:                 task_ids.add(task_id)

 7353:                     invalid("terminal state commit parent does not match its recorded base")
 7354:                 if git(Path(config.repo), "rev-parse", f"{commit}^{{tree}}").stdout.strip() != candidate_id:
 7355:                     invalid("terminal state commit tree does not match its recorded candidate")

 7359:                 invalid("terminal state latest commit is not bound to completed history")
 7360:             if state.get("current_task_id") is not None:
 7361:                 invalid("terminal state retains a current task")
 7362:             if any(state.get(key) is not None for key in ("candidate_id", "pending_commit", "active_attempt_ref")):
 7363:                 invalid("terminal state retains an ambiguous candidate or attempt")

 8956:     def execute(self, identifier: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
 8957:         run_dir = self._run_dir(identifier)
 8958:         with _run_state_lock(run_dir):
 8959:             return self._execute_locked(identifier)
 8960: 
 8961:     def _execute_locked(self, identifier: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
 8962:         """Run the validated master plan without routine planner calls."""
 8963:         run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
 8964:         if state["phase"] in {"committed", "complete"}:
 8965:             return self._validate_terminal_state(identifier, run_dir, state, config)
 8966:         if state["phase"] == "blocked":
 8967:             raise FactoryError(
 8968:                 "blocked run requires a resolved condition or explicit authority before resume",
 8969:                 "blocked",
 8970:             )
 8971:         plan = self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
 8972:         while True:
 8973:             run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
 8974:             if state["phase"] in {"committed", "complete"}:
 8975:                 return self._validate_terminal_state(identifier, run_dir, state, config)
 8976:             plan = self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
 8977:             if state["phase"] == "committing" or state.get("pending_commit") is not None:
 8978:                 try:
 8979:                     self._reconcile_pending_commit(identifier, run_dir, state, config, plan)
 8980:                 except FactoryError as exc:
 8981:                     self._save(
 8982:                         run_dir,
 8983:                         state,
 8984:                         "blocked",
 8985:                         block_reason=str(exc),
 8986:                         error=str(exc),
 8987:                         error_category="blocked",
 8988:                     )
 8989:                     raise
 8990:                 continue
 8991:             if state["phase"] in {"verifying", "reviewing"}:
 8992:                 try:
 8993:                     self._resume_existing_task_phase(identifier, run_dir, state, config, plan)
 8994:                 except ReplanRequested as exc:
 8995:                     run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
 8996:                     reason_ref = self._persist_replan_reason(run_dir, state, exc.reason)
 8997:                     ledger = MemoryLedger(
 8998:                         run_dir,
 8999:                         identifier,
 9000:                         config.repo,
 9001:                         state.get("objective_class", "capability_growth"),
 9002:                     )
 9003:                     self._perform_planning(
 9004:                         run_dir,
 9005:                         state,
 9006:                         config,
 9007:                         self._repo(config.repo),
 9008:                         ledger=ledger,
 9009:                         replan_reason=json.dumps(exc.reason, sort_keys=True),
 9010:                         planning_repo=(
 9011:                             Path(state["worktree"])
 9012:                             if isinstance(state.get("worktree"), str)
 9013:                             and Path(state["worktree"]).is_dir()
 9014:                             else None
 9015:                         ),
 9016:                     )
 9017:                     continue
 9018:                 except FactoryError as exc:
 9019:                     if state.get("phase") != "blocked":
 9020:                         self._save(
 9021:                             run_dir,
 9022:                             state,
 9023:                             "blocked",
 9024:                             block_reason=str(exc),
 9025:                             error=str(exc),
 9026:                             error_category="blocked",
 9027:                         )
 9028:                     raise
 9029:                 continue
 9030:             if state["phase"] == "planning" and state.get("replan_reason_ref"):
 9031:                 reason_ref = state["replan_reason_ref"]
 9032:                 if not isinstance(reason_ref, str) or not re.fullmatch(
 9033:                     r"replan_reason_[0-9]+\.json", reason_ref
 9034:                 ):
 9035:                     raise FactoryError("planning state has an invalid replan reason reference", "blocked")
 9036:                 reason_path = (run_dir / reason_ref).resolve()
 9037:                 try:
 9038:                     reason_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 9039:                 except ValueError as exc:
 9040:                     raise FactoryError("planning state replan reason escapes the run directory", "blocked") from exc
 9041:                 if not reason_path.is_file():
 9042:                     raise FactoryError("planning state replan reason artifact is missing", "blocked")
 9043:                 reason = validate_replan_reason(read_json(reason_path))
 9044:                 ledger = MemoryLedger(
 9045:                     run_dir,
 9046:                     identifier,
 9047:                     config.repo,
 9048:                     state.get("objective_class", "capability_growth"),
 9049:                 )
 9050:                 self._perform_planning(
 9051:                     run_dir,
 9052:                     state,
 9053:                     config,
 9054:                     self._repo(config.repo),
 9055:                     ledger=ledger,
 9056:                     replan_reason=json.dumps(reason, sort_keys=True),
 9057:                     planning_repo=(
 9058:                         Path(state["worktree"])
 9059:                         if isinstance(state.get("worktree"), str)
 9060:                         and Path(state["worktree"]).is_dir()
 9061:                         else None
 9062:                     ),
 9063:                 )
 9064:                 continue
 9065: 
 9066:             completed_commits = state.get("completed_commits")
 9067:             if not isinstance(completed_commits, list):

 9160:                     },

 9274:         run_dir = self._run_dir(identifier)
 9275:         with _run_state_lock(run_dir):

 9278:     def _resume_locked(self, identifier: str) -> dict[str, Any]:

 9282:         if state["phase"] == "blocked" and state.get("error_category") == "blocked":

 9286:             )

 9307:             )

 6841:     def _validate_replan_history(self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
 6842:         reason_paths = sorted(
 6843:             (

 6849:         )
 6850:         if not reason_paths:

 6917:     def _block_replan_exhaustion(
 6918:         self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], *, attempts: int, reason: dict[str, Any] | None = None
 6919:     ) -> None:
 6920:         block_reason = "exceptional replan budget exhausted; run is blocked"

 6924:             "last_reason": reason,
 6925:             "last_reason_ref": state.get("replan_reason_ref"),
 6926:         }
 6927:         atomic_json(run_dir / "replan_blocked.json", evidence)
 6928:         self._save(
 6929:             run_dir,
 6930:             state,
 6931:             "blocked",
 6932:             block_reason=block_reason,
 6933:             error=block_reason,
 6934:             error_category="blocked",
 6935:             replan_block_evidence="replan_blocked.json",
 6936:         )

 6939:     def _persist_replan_reason(
 6940:         self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], reason: dict[str, Any]
 6941:     ) -> str:
 6942:         reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
 6943:         artifact_ref = reason["evidence"]["artifact_ref"]
 6944:         artifact_path = (run_dir / artifact_ref).resolve()
 6945:         try:
 6946:             artifact_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 6947:         except ValueError as exc:
 6948:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence reference escapes the run directory", "blocked") from exc
 6949:         if not artifact_path.is_file():
 6950:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence artifact is missing", "blocked")
 6951:         evidence_hash = self._validate_replan_evidence(run_dir, reason, state)
 6952:         reason["evidence"]["artifact_sha256"] = evidence_hash
 6953:         attempt = self._durable_replan_attempt_count(run_dir, state) + 1
 6954:         if self._durable_replan_dispatch_count(run_dir) >= 2:
 6955:             self._block_replan_exhaustion(
 6956:                 run_dir,
 6957:                 state,
 6958:                 attempts=self._durable_replan_dispatch_count(run_dir),
 6959:                 reason=reason,
 6960:             )
 6961:         reason_ref = f"replan_reason_{attempt}.json"
 6962:         reason_path = run_dir / reason_ref
 6963:         if reason_path.exists():
 6964:             raise FactoryError("replan reason history is ambiguous", "blocked")
 6965:         atomic_json(reason_path, reason)
 6966:         self._save(run_dir, state, "planning", replan_reason_ref=reason_ref)
 6967:         return reason_ref

 7221:         elif existing.get("commit") != commit_hash or existing.get("candidate_id") != expected_candidate:
 7222:             raise FactoryError("completed task history conflicts with pending commit", "blocked")
 7223:         state["completed_commits"] = completed_commits
 7224:         state["base_commit"] = commit_hash
 7225:         state["candidate_id"] = None
 7226:         state["pending_commit"] = None
 7227:         state["active_attempt_ref"] = None
 7228:         task_evidence = next(
 7229:             (
 7230:                 item
 7231:                 for item in (state.get("criterion_evidence") or {}).values()
 7232:                 if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("task_id") == task_id
 7233:             ),
 7234:             None,
 7235:         )
 7236:         if (
 7237:             not isinstance(task_evidence, dict)
 7238:             or not isinstance(task_evidence.get("review_sha256"), str)
 7239:             or not isinstance(task_evidence.get("verification_sha256"), str)
 7240:             or not isinstance(task_evidence.get("verification_sequence"), int)
 7241:         ):
 7242:             raise FactoryError("pending commit lacks bound verification provenance", "blocked")
 7243:         self._record_approved_criteria(
 7244:             state,
 7245:             task,
 7246:             review,
 7247:             base_commit=base_commit,
 7248:             candidate_id=expected_candidate,
 7249:             review_ref=str(state.get("review_ref")),
 7250:             verification_ref=state.get("verification_ref"),
 7251:             review_sha256=task_evidence["review_sha256"],
 7252:             verification_sha256=task_evidence["verification_sha256"],
 7253:             verification_sequence=task_evidence["verification_sequence"],
 7254:             review_criteria_ids=task_evidence.get("review_criteria_ids"),
 7255:         )
 7256:         self._record_committed_task_criteria(state, master_plan, task)
 7257:         self._bind_criteria_to_commit(state, task_id, commit_hash)
 7258:         self._save(
 7259:             run_dir,
 7260:             state,
 7261:             "planned",
 7262:             commit=commit_hash,
 7263:             current_task_id=task_id,
 7264:             completed_commits=completed_commits,
 7265:             criterion_evidence=state["criterion_evidence"],
 7266:             pending_commit=None,
 7267:             candidate_id=None,
 7268:             candidate_state_identity=None,
 7269:             active_attempt_ref=None,
 7270:         )
 7271:         return state

 7281:         def invalid(reason: str) -> None:

 7287:                 error=reason,

 7289:             )

 7293:             plan = self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)

 7295:             if replan_reason_ref is not None:
 7296:                 if not isinstance(replan_reason_ref, str) or not re.fullmatch(
 7297:                     r"replan_reason_[0-9]+\.json", replan_reason_ref
 7298:                 ):

 7303:                 except ValueError:

 7305:                 if not replan_path.is_file():
 7306:                     invalid("terminal state replan reason artifact is missing")

 7310:                     invalid("terminal state replan evidence reference is invalid")
 7311:                 evidence_path = (run_dir / evidence_ref).resolve()

 7313:                     evidence_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 7314:                 except ValueError:

 7326:             completed = state.get("completed_commits")
 7327:             if not isinstance(completed, list) or not completed:
 7328:                 invalid("terminal state has no completed commit history")
 7329:             task_ids: set[str] = set()
 7330:             plan_ids = {task["id"] for task in tasks}
 7331:             for record in completed:
 7332:                 if not isinstance(record, dict):
 7333:                     invalid("terminal state has an invalid completed commit record")

 7335:                 commit = record.get("commit")
 7336:                 candidate_id = record.get("candidate_id")

 7344:                     or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", candidate_id)
 7345:                     or not isinstance(base_commit, str)
 7346:                     or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", base_commit)

 5616:     def _scope_check(self, worktree: Path, allowed: list[str], label: str, run_dir: Path) -> list[str]:
 5617:         # Revalidate after every builder/test boundary. A builder can replace a
 5618:         # previously safe file with a symlink before staging.

 6917:     def _block_replan_exhaustion(
 6918:         self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], *, attempts: int, reason: dict[str, Any] | None = None
 6919:     ) -> None:
 6920:         block_reason = "exceptional replan budget exhausted; run is blocked"
 6921:         evidence = {
 6922:             "reason": block_reason,
 6923:             "replan_attempts": attempts,
 6924:             "last_reason": reason,
 6925:             "last_reason_ref": state.get("replan_reason_ref"),
 6926:         }
 6927:         atomic_json(run_dir / "replan_blocked.json", evidence)
 6928:         self._save(
 6929:             run_dir,
 6930:             state,
 6931:             "blocked",
 6932:             block_reason=block_reason,
 6933:             error=block_reason,
 6934:             error_category="blocked",
 6935:             replan_block_evidence="replan_blocked.json",
 6936:         )
 6937:         raise FactoryError(block_reason, "blocked")

 6939:     def _persist_replan_reason(
 6940:         self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], reason: dict[str, Any]
 6941:     ) -> str:
 6942:         reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
 6943:         artifact_ref = reason["evidence"]["artifact_ref"]
 6944:         artifact_path = (run_dir / artifact_ref).resolve()
 6945:         try:
 6946:             artifact_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 6947:         except ValueError as exc:
 6948:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence reference escapes the run directory", "blocked") from exc
 6949:         if not artifact_path.is_file():
 6950:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence artifact is missing", "blocked")
 6951:         evidence_hash = self._validate_replan_evidence(run_dir, reason, state)
 6952:         reason["evidence"]["artifact_sha256"] = evidence_hash
 6953:         attempt = self._durable_replan_attempt_count(run_dir, state) + 1
 6954:         if self._durable_replan_dispatch_count(run_dir) >= 2:
 6955:             self._block_replan_exhaustion(
 6956:                 run_dir,
 6957:                 state,
 6958:                 attempts=self._durable_replan_dispatch_count(run_dir),
 6959:                 reason=reason,
 6960:             )
 6961:         reason_ref = f"replan_reason_{attempt}.json"
 6962:         reason_path = run_dir / reason_ref
 6963:         if reason_path.exists():
 6964:             raise FactoryError("replan reason history is ambiguous", "blocked")
 6965:         atomic_json(reason_path, reason)
 6966:         self._save(run_dir, state, "planning", replan_reason_ref=reason_ref)
 6967:         return reason_ref

 6969:     def request_replan(
 6970:         self,
 6971:         identifier: str,
 6972:         reason: dict[str, Any],
 6973:         *,
 6974:         objective: str | None = None,
 6975:         requested_files: list[str] | None = None,
 6976:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 6977:         """Accept only a verified structured strategic trigger for an Astra replan."""
 6978:         normalized_reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
 6979:         run_dir = self._run_dir(identifier)
 6980:         with _run_state_lock(run_dir):
 6981:             run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
 6982:             if state["phase"] in {"committed", "complete"}:
 6983:                 return self._validate_terminal_state(identifier, run_dir, state, config)
 6984:             if state.get("candidate_id") is not None or state.get("pending_commit") is not None:
 6985:                 raise FactoryError("cannot replan with an ambiguous candidate or pending commit", "blocked")
 6986:             self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
 6987:             if normalized_reason["code"] == "USER_OBJECTIVE_OR_SCOPE_CHANGE":
 6988:                 if not isinstance(objective, str) or not objective.strip() or requested_files is None:
 6989:                     raise FactoryError(
 6990:                         "user objective/scope replan requires replacement objective and authorized files",
 6991:                         "input",
 6992:                     )
 6993:                 replacement_files = normalized_paths(requested_files)
 6994:                 repo = self._repo(config.repo)
 6995:                 ensure_paths_confined(repo, replacement_files)
 6996:                 ensure_head_paths_not_symlinks(repo, replacement_files)
 6997:                 config = replace(config, request=objective.strip(), requested_files=replacement_files)
 6998:                 state["config"] = asdict(config)
 6999:                 change_ref = f"request_change_{uuid.uuid4().hex}.json"
 7000:                 normalized_reason["evidence"] = dict(normalized_reason["evidence"])
 7001:                 normalized_reason["evidence"]["artifact_ref"] = change_ref
 7002:                 atomic_json(
 7003:                     run_dir / change_ref,
 7004:                     {
 7005:                         "request": config.request,
 7006:                         "requested_files": replacement_files,
 7007:                         "reason": normalized_reason,
 7008:                     },
 7009:                 )
 7010:                 state["request_ref"] = change_ref
 7011:                 self._save(run_dir, state, state["phase"], request_change_ref=change_ref)
 7012:             elif objective is not None or requested_files is not None:
 7013:                 raise FactoryError(
 7014:                     "replacement objective/scope is allowed only for USER_OBJECTIVE_OR_SCOPE_CHANGE",
 7015:                     "input",
 7016:                 )
 7017:             reason_ref = self._persist_replan_reason(run_dir, state, normalized_reason)
 7018:             ledger = MemoryLedger(
 7019:                 run_dir,
 7020:                 identifier,
 7021:                 config.repo,
 7022:                 state.get("objective_class", "capability_growth"),
 7023:             )
 7024:             self._perform_planning(
 7025:                 run_dir,
 7026:                 state,
 7027:                 config,
 7028:                 self._repo(config.repo),
 7029:                 ledger=ledger,
 7030:                 replan_reason=json.dumps(normalized_reason, sort_keys=True),
 7031:                 planning_repo=(

 7042:     def _reconcile_pending_commit(

 7044:         identifier: str,

 7042:     def _reconcile_pending_commit(
 7043:         self,
 7044:         identifier: str,
 7045:         run_dir: Path,
 7046:         state: dict[str, Any],
 7047:         config: Config,
 7048:         master_plan: dict[str, Any],
 7049:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 7050:         pending = state.get("pending_commit")
 7051:         if not isinstance(pending, dict) or not isinstance(pending.get("candidate_id"), str):
 7052:             raise FactoryError("committing state has no valid pending candidate", "blocked")
 7053:         expected_candidate = pending["candidate_id"]
 7054:         task_id = state.get("current_task_id")
 7055:         if not isinstance(task_id, str):
 7056:             raise FactoryError("committing state has no current task", "blocked")
 7057:         task = next((item for item in master_plan_tasks(master_plan) if item["id"] == task_id), None)
 7058:         if task is None:
 7059:             raise FactoryError("pending commit task is not present in the master plan", "blocked")
 7060:         worktree_raw = state.get("worktree")
 7061:         if not isinstance(worktree_raw, str) or not Path(worktree_raw).is_dir():
 7062:             raise FactoryError("pending commit worktree is missing", "blocked")
 7063:         worktree = Path(worktree_raw)
 7064:         base_commit = state.get("base_commit")
 7065:         if not isinstance(base_commit, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", base_commit):
 7066:             raise FactoryError("pending commit has an invalid base commit", "blocked")
 7067:         review_ref = state.get("review_ref")
 7068:         if not isinstance(review_ref, str) or not review_ref:
 7069:             raise FactoryError("pending commit has no review reference", "blocked")
 7070:         review_path = (run_dir / review_ref).resolve()
 7071:         try:
 7072:             review_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 7073:         except ValueError as exc:
 7074:             raise FactoryError("pending review reference escapes the run directory", "blocked") from exc
 7075:         if not review_path.is_file():
 7076:             raise FactoryError("pending review artifact is missing", "blocked")
 7077:         review = read_json(review_path)
 7078:         if state.get("candidate_id") != expected_candidate:
 7079:             raise FactoryError("pending commit candidate is not bound to run state", "blocked")
 7080:         pending_base = pending.get("base_commit")
 7081:         if pending_base != base_commit:
 7082:             raise FactoryError("pending commit base does not match the approved base", "blocked")
 7083:         pending_review_hash = pending.get("reviewed_diff_sha256")
 7084:         if pending_review_hash != review.get("reviewed_diff_sha256"):
 7085:             raise FactoryError("pending commit review hash is not bound to the approval", "blocked")
 7086:         if pending.get("review_ref") != review_ref:
 7087:             raise FactoryError("pending commit review reference is not bound to the approval", "blocked")
 7088:         pending_candidate_state = pending.get("candidate_state_identity")
 7089:         state_candidate_state = state.get("candidate_state_identity")
 7090:         if (
 7091:             not isinstance(pending_candidate_state, dict)
 7092:             or not isinstance(state_candidate_state, dict)
 7093:             or pending_candidate_state.get("complete_identity_sha256")
 7094:             != state_candidate_state.get("complete_identity_sha256")
 7095:         ):
 7096:             raise FactoryError("pending commit candidate state is not bound to the approval", "blocked")
 7097:         if pending.get("verification_ref") != state.get("verification_ref"):
 7098:             raise FactoryError("pending commit verification reference is not bound to the approval", "blocked")
 7099:         verification_ref = state.get("verification_ref")
 7100:         verification_results = state.get("test_results")
 7101:         if (
 7102:             not isinstance(verification_ref, str)
 7103:             or not isinstance(verification_results, list)
 7104:             or not verification_results
 7105:             or not isinstance(verification_results[-1], dict)
 7106:         ):
 7107:             raise FactoryError("pending commit has no verification provenance", "blocked")
 7108:         verification_value, verification_sha256 = self._bind_verification_artifact(
 7109:             run_dir,
 7110:             verification_ref,
 7111:             task_id=task_id,
 7112:             base_commit=base_commit,
 7113:             candidate_id=expected_candidate,
 7114:             expected_result=verification_results[-1],
 7115:         )
 7116:         if pending.get("verification_sha256") != verification_sha256:
 7117:             raise FactoryError("pending commit verification digest is not bound to the approval", "blocked")
 7118:         review_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(review_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
 7119:         if pending.get("review_sha256") != review_sha256:
 7120:             raise FactoryError("pending commit review artifact integrity is stale", "blocked")
 7121:         verification_contract = self._verification_contract(run_dir, verification_ref)
 7122:         if review.get("verification") != verification_contract:
 7123:             raise FactoryError("pending commit verification is not bound to the approval", "blocked")
 7124: 
 7125:         head = git(worktree, "rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout.strip()
 7126:         if head != base_commit:
 7127:             parent = git(worktree, "rev-parse", "HEAD^").stdout.strip()
 7128:             tree = git(worktree, "rev-parse", "HEAD^{tree}").stdout.strip()
 7129:             if parent != base_commit or tree != expected_candidate:
 7130:                 raise FactoryError("pending commit does not match the approved base/tree", "blocked")
 7131:             post_commit_state = AgyBuilder._candidate_baseline_identity(worktree)
 7132:             if (
 7133:                 post_commit_state.get("head") != head
 7134:                 or post_commit_state.get("tree") != expected_candidate
 7135:                 or post_commit_state.get("status_records")
 7136:                 or post_commit_state.get("staged_diff_sha256")
 7137:                 != hashlib.sha256(b"").hexdigest()
 7138:                 or post_commit_state.get("unstaged_diff_sha256")
 7139:                 != hashlib.sha256(b"").hexdigest()
 7140:                 or not post_commit_state["clean"]
 7141:             ):
 7142:                 raise FactoryError(
 7143:                     "pending commit has post-approval candidate mutations",
 7144:                     "blocked",
 7145:                 )
 7146:             committed_diff = git(
 7147:                 worktree, "diff", f"{base_commit}..{head}", "--binary", "--no-ext-diff"
 7148:             ).stdout
 7149:             validate_review(
 7150:                 review,
 7151:                 hashlib.sha256(committed_diff.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
 7152:                 base_commit,
 7153:                 expected_candidate,
 7154:                 list(task.get("global_criteria", []))
 7155:                 + [item["id"] for item in task.get("acceptance_criteria", [])],
 7156:                 verification_contract,
 7157:             )
 7158:             if review.get("decision") != "APPROVE":
 7159:                 raise FactoryError("pending commit reconciliation requires Sol APPROVE", "blocked")
 7160:             commit_hash = head
 7161:         else:

 7163:             current_hash = hashlib.sha256(staged_diff.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

 7165:             unstaged_diff = git(worktree, "diff", "--binary", "--no-ext-diff").stdout
 7166:             current_candidate_state = AgyBuilder._candidate_baseline_identity(worktree)
 7167:             reviewed_candidate_state = state.get("candidate_state_identity")
 7168:             validate_review(
 7169:                 review,
 7170:                 current_hash,
 7171:                 base_commit,
 7172:                 expected_candidate,
 7173:                 list(task.get("global_criteria", []))
 7174:                 + [item["id"] for item in task.get("acceptance_criteria", [])],
 7175:                 verification_contract,
 7176:             )
 7177:             if review.get("decision") != "APPROVE":
 7178:                 raise FactoryError("pending commit reconciliation requires Sol APPROVE", "blocked")
 7179:             if current_tree != expected_candidate or unstaged_diff:
 7180:                 raise FactoryError("pending candidate changed before commit reconciliation", "blocked")
 7181:             if (
 7182:                 not isinstance(reviewed_candidate_state, dict)
 7183:                 or current_candidate_state.get("complete_identity_sha256")
 7184:                 != reviewed_candidate_state.get("complete_identity_sha256")
 7185:             ):
 7186:                 raise FactoryError("pending candidate state changed before commit reconciliation", "blocked")
 7187:             message = (
 7188:                 f"hermes: {task['objective'].strip()[:68]}\n\n"
 7189:                 f"Factory-Run: {identifier}\n"
 7190:                 f"Sol-Model: {config.sol_model}\n"

 7192:                 f"Reviewed-Diff-SHA256: {current_hash}\n"
 7193:                 f"Candidate-Tree: {expected_candidate}"
 7194:             )

 7196:             if committed.returncode != 0:
 7197:                 raise FactoryError(
 7198:                     f"pending commit reconciliation failed: {(committed.stderr or committed.stdout).strip()}",
 7199:                     "blocked",
 7200:                 )
 7201:             commit_hash = git(worktree, "rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout.strip()
 7202:             if git(worktree, "rev-parse", "HEAD^{tree}").stdout.strip() != expected_candidate:
 7203:                 raise FactoryError("reconciled commit tree does not match the pending candidate", "blocked")
 7204: 
 7205:         completed_commits = state.get("completed_commits")
 7206:         if not isinstance(completed_commits, list):
 7207:             raise FactoryError("run completed_commits state is invalid", "state")
 7208:         completed_commits = list(completed_commits)
 7209:         existing = next(
 7210:             (item for item in completed_commits if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("task_id") == task_id),
 7211:             None,
 7212:         )
 7213:         completed_record = {
 7214:             "task_id": task_id,
 7215:             "commit": commit_hash,
 7216:             "candidate_id": expected_candidate,
 7217:             "base_commit": base_commit,
 7218:         }
 7219:         if existing is None:
 7220:             completed_commits.append(completed_record)
 7221:         elif existing.get("commit") != commit_hash or existing.get("candidate_id") != expected_candidate:
 7222:             raise FactoryError("completed task history conflicts with pending commit", "blocked")
 7223:         state["completed_commits"] = completed_commits
 7224:         state["base_commit"] = commit_hash
 7225:         state["candidate_id"] = None
 7226:         state["pending_commit"] = None
 7227:         state["active_attempt_ref"] = None
 7228:         task_evidence = next(
 7229:             (

 7235:         )
 7236:         if (
 7237:             not isinstance(task_evidence, dict)
 7238:             or not isinstance(task_evidence.get("review_sha256"), str)
 7239:             or not isinstance(task_evidence.get("verification_sha256"), str)
 7240:             or not isinstance(task_evidence.get("verification_sequence"), int)
 7241:         ):
 7242:             raise FactoryError("pending commit lacks bound verification provenance", "blocked")
 7243:         self._record_approved_criteria(
 7244:             state,
 7245:             task,
 7246:             review,
 7247:             base_commit=base_commit,
 7248:             candidate_id=expected_candidate,
 7249:             review_ref=str(state.get("review_ref")),

 7260:             state,

 7262:             commit=commit_hash,
 7263:             current_task_id=task_id,

 7273:     def _validate_terminal_state(
 7274:         self,
 7275:         identifier: str,
 7276:         run_dir: Path,
 7277:         state: dict[str, Any],
 7278:         config: Config,
 7279:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 7280:         """Revalidate a terminal snapshot before trusting its fast path."""
 7281:         def invalid(reason: str) -> None:
 7282:             self._save(
 7283:                 run_dir,
 7284:                 state,
 7285:                 "blocked",
 7286:                 block_reason=reason,
 7287:                 error=reason,
 7288:                 error_category="blocked",
 7289:             )
 7290:             raise FactoryError(reason, "blocked")
 7291: 
 7292:         try:
 7293:             plan = self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
 7294:             replan_reason_ref = state.get("replan_reason_ref")
 7295:             if replan_reason_ref is not None:
 7296:                 if not isinstance(replan_reason_ref, str) or not re.fullmatch(
 7297:                     r"replan_reason_[0-9]+\.json", replan_reason_ref
 7298:                 ):
 7299:                     invalid("terminal state has an invalid replan reason reference")
 7300:                 replan_path = (run_dir / replan_reason_ref).resolve()
 7301:                 try:
 7302:                     replan_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 7303:                 except ValueError:
 7304:                     invalid("terminal state replan reason escapes the run")
 7305:                 if not replan_path.is_file():
 7306:                     invalid("terminal state replan reason artifact is missing")
 7307:                 persisted_reason = validate_replan_reason(read_json(replan_path))
 7308:                 evidence_ref = persisted_reason["evidence"].get("artifact_ref")
 7309:                 if not isinstance(evidence_ref, str):
 7310:                     invalid("terminal state replan evidence reference is invalid")
 7311:                 evidence_path = (run_dir / evidence_ref).resolve()
 7312:                 try:
 7313:                     evidence_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 7314:                 except ValueError:
 7315:                     invalid("terminal state replan evidence escapes the run")
 7316:                 recorded_hash = persisted_reason["evidence"].get("artifact_sha256")
 7317:                 if (
 7318:                     not evidence_path.is_file()
 7319:                     or not isinstance(recorded_hash, str)
 7320:                     or hashlib.sha256(evidence_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != recorded_hash
 7321:                 ):
 7322:                     invalid("terminal state replan evidence integrity is stale")
 7323:                 if evidence_ref.startswith("sol_review_"):
 7324:                     self._validate_replan_evidence(run_dir, persisted_reason, state, historical=True)
 7325:             tasks = master_plan_tasks(plan)
 7326:             completed = state.get("completed_commits")
 7327:             if not isinstance(completed, list) or not completed:
 7328:                 invalid("terminal state has no completed commit history")
 7329:             task_ids: set[str] = set()
 7330:             plan_ids = {task["id"] for task in tasks}
 7331:             for record in completed:
 7332:                 if not isinstance(record, dict):
 7333:                     invalid("terminal state has an invalid completed commit record")
 7334:                 task_id = record.get("task_id")
 7335:                 commit = record.get("commit")
 7336:                 candidate_id = record.get("candidate_id")
 7337:                 base_commit = record.get("base_commit")
 7338:                 if (
 7339:                     not isinstance(task_id, str)
 7340:                     or task_id in task_ids
 7341:                     or not isinstance(commit, str)
 7342:                     or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", commit)
 7343:                     or not isinstance(candidate_id, str)
 7344:                     or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", candidate_id)
 7345:                     or not isinstance(base_commit, str)
 7346:                     or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40}", base_commit)
 7347:                 ):
 7348:                     invalid("terminal state has an invalid or duplicate completed commit record")
 7349:                 if task_id not in plan_ids:
 7350:                     invalid("terminal state references a task absent from the master plan")
 7351:                 task_ids.add(task_id)
 7352:                 if git(Path(config.repo), "rev-parse", f"{commit}^").stdout.strip() != base_commit:
 7353:                     invalid("terminal state commit parent does not match its recorded base")
 7354:                 if git(Path(config.repo), "rev-parse", f"{commit}^{{tree}}").stdout.strip() != candidate_id:
 7355:                     invalid("terminal state commit tree does not match its recorded candidate")
 7356:             if task_ids != plan_ids:
 7357:                 invalid("terminal state is missing a committed master-plan task")
 7358:             if state.get("commit") != completed[-1].get("commit"):
 7359:                 invalid("terminal state latest commit is not bound to completed history")
 7360:             if state.get("current_task_id") is not None:
 7361:                 invalid("terminal state retains a current task")
 7362:             if any(state.get(key) is not None for key in ("candidate_id", "pending_commit", "active_attempt_ref")):
 7363:                 invalid("terminal state retains an ambiguous candidate or attempt")
 7364:             gaps = self._remaining_master_plan_gaps(plan, state)
 7365:             if gaps:
 7366:                 invalid(f"terminal state has incomplete global criterion evidence: {gaps}")
 7367:             evidence = state.get("criterion_evidence")
 7368:             if not isinstance(evidence, dict):
 7369:                 invalid("terminal state criterion evidence is missing")
 7370:             completed_by_commit = {
 7371:                 item["commit"]: item
 7372:                 for item in completed
 7373:                 if isinstance(item, dict) and isinstance(item.get("commit"), str)
 7374:             }
 7375:             task_by_id = {task["id"]: task for task in tasks}
 7376:             validated_reviews: set[str] = set()
 7377:             for criterion_id, item in evidence.items():
 7378:                 if not isinstance(item, dict) or item.get("status") != "PASS":
 7379:                     continue
 7380:                 commit_record = completed_by_commit.get(item.get("commit"))
