 7382:                     invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} has stale commit lineage")
 7383:                 task = task_by_id.get(item.get("task_id"))
 7384:                 if task is None:
 7385:                     invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} has an unknown task")
 7386:                 for field in ("review_ref", "verification_ref"):
 7387:                     reference = item.get(field)
 7388:                     if not isinstance(reference, str) or not reference:
 7389:                         invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} has no {field}")
 7390:                     artifact = (run_dir / reference).resolve()
 7391:                     try:
 7392:                         artifact.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 7393:                     except ValueError:
 7394:                         invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} has an escaping {field}")
 7395:                     if not artifact.is_file():
 7396:                         invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} references missing {field}")
 7397:                     artifact_value = read_json(artifact)
 7398:                     if not isinstance(artifact_value, dict):
 7399:                         invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} references malformed {field}")
 7400:                     recorded_hash = item.get(
 7401:                         "review_sha256" if field == "review_ref" else "verification_sha256"
 7402:                     )
 7403:                     if (
 7404:                         not isinstance(recorded_hash, str)
 7405:                         or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", recorded_hash)
 7406:                         or hashlib.sha256(artifact.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != recorded_hash
 7407:                     ):
 7408:                         invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} {field} integrity is stale")
 7409:                     if field == "review_ref" and (
 7410:                         artifact_value.get("decision") != "APPROVE"
 7411:                     ):
 7412:                         invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} references a non-approval review")
 7413:                     if field == "review_ref" and (
 7414:                         artifact_value.get("base_commit") != item.get("base_commit")
 7415:                         or artifact_value.get("candidate_id") != item.get("candidate_id")
 7416:                     ):
 7417:                         invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} review identity is stale")
 7418:                     if field == "verification_ref" and (
 7419:                         artifact_value.get("passed") is not True
 7420:                         or artifact_value.get("returncode") != 0
 7421:                         or not isinstance(artifact_value.get("command"), str)
 7422:                     ):
 7423:                         invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} references failed verification")
 7424:                     if field == "verification_ref":
 7425:                         verification_match = re.fullmatch(r"test_([0-9]+)\.json", reference)
 7426:                         if not verification_match or artifact_value.get("artifact_sequence") != int(verification_match.group(1)):
 7427:                             invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} verification sequence is stale")
 7428:                         configured_command = config.test_command
 7429:                         if configured_command is not None and artifact_value.get("command") != normalize_test_command(configured_command):
 7430:                             invalid(f"terminal state criterion {criterion_id} verification command is stale")

 7437:                         except ValueError:

 7447:                         if artifact_value.get("sequence") != item.get("verification_sequence"):

 7452:                     review_value = read_json(review_path)

 6939:     def _persist_replan_reason(
 6940:         self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], reason: dict[str, Any]
 6941:     ) -> str:
 6942:         reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
 6943:         artifact_ref = reason["evidence"]["artifact_ref"]
 6944:         artifact_path = (run_dir / artifact_ref).resolve()
 6945:         try:
 6946:             artifact_path.relative_to(run_dir.resolve())
 6947:         except ValueError as exc:
 6948:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence reference escapes the run directory", "blocked") from exc
 6949:         if not artifact_path.is_file():
 6950:             raise FactoryError("replan evidence artifact is missing", "blocked")
 6951:         evidence_hash = self._validate_replan_evidence(run_dir, reason, state)
 6952:         reason["evidence"]["artifact_sha256"] = evidence_hash
 6953:         attempt = self._durable_replan_attempt_count(run_dir, state) + 1
 6954:         if self._durable_replan_dispatch_count(run_dir) >= 2:
 6955:             self._block_replan_exhaustion(
 6956:                 run_dir,
 6957:                 state,
 6958:                 attempts=self._durable_replan_dispatch_count(run_dir),
 6959:                 reason=reason,
 6960:             )
 6961:         reason_ref = f"replan_reason_{attempt}.json"
 6962:         reason_path = run_dir / reason_ref
 6963:         if reason_path.exists():
 6964:             raise FactoryError("replan reason history is ambiguous", "blocked")
 6965:         atomic_json(reason_path, reason)
 6966:         self._save(run_dir, state, "planning", replan_reason_ref=reason_ref)
 6967:         return reason_ref

 6969:     def request_replan(
 6970:         self,
 6971:         identifier: str,
 6972:         reason: dict[str, Any],
 6973:         *,
 6974:         objective: str | None = None,
 6975:         requested_files: list[str] | None = None,
 6976:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 6977:         """Accept only a verified structured strategic trigger for an Astra replan."""
 6978:         normalized_reason = validate_replan_reason(reason)
 6979:         run_dir = self._run_dir(identifier)
 6980:         with _run_state_lock(run_dir):
 6981:             run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
 6982:             if state["phase"] in {"committed", "complete"}:
 6983:                 return self._validate_terminal_state(identifier, run_dir, state, config)
 6984:             if state.get("candidate_id") is not None or state.get("pending_commit") is not None:
 6985:                 raise FactoryError("cannot replan with an ambiguous candidate or pending commit", "blocked")
 6986:             self._load_master_plan_for_execution(run_dir, config, state)
 6987:             if normalized_reason["code"] == "USER_OBJECTIVE_OR_SCOPE_CHANGE":
 6988:                 if not isinstance(objective, str) or not objective.strip() or requested_files is None:
 6989:                     raise FactoryError(
 6990:                         "user objective/scope replan requires replacement objective and authorized files",
 6991:                         "input",
 6992:                     )
 6993:                 replacement_files = normalized_paths(requested_files)
 6994:                 repo = self._repo(config.repo)
 6995:                 ensure_paths_confined(repo, replacement_files)
 6996:                 ensure_head_paths_not_symlinks(repo, replacement_files)
 6997:                 config = replace(config, request=objective.strip(), requested_files=replacement_files)
 6998:                 state["config"] = asdict(config)
 6999:                 change_ref = f"request_change_{uuid.uuid4().hex}.json"
 7000:                 normalized_reason["evidence"] = dict(normalized_reason["evidence"])
 7001:                 normalized_reason["evidence"]["artifact_ref"] = change_ref
 7002:                 atomic_json(
 7003:                     run_dir / change_ref,
 7004:                     {
 7005:                         "request": config.request,
 7006:                         "requested_files": replacement_files,
 7007:                         "reason": normalized_reason,
 7008:                     },
 7009:                 )

 7011:                 self._save(run_dir, state, state["phase"], request_change_ref=change_ref)
 7012:             elif objective is not None or requested_files is not None:
 7013:                 raise FactoryError(
 7014:                     "replacement objective/scope is allowed only for USER_OBJECTIVE_OR_SCOPE_CHANGE",
 7015:                     "input",
 7016:                 )
 7017:             reason_ref = self._persist_replan_reason(run_dir, state, normalized_reason)
 7018:             ledger = MemoryLedger(
 7019:                 run_dir,
 7020:                 identifier,
 7021:                 config.repo,
 7022:                 state.get("objective_class", "capability_growth"),
 7023:             )
 7024:             self._perform_planning(
 7025:                 run_dir,
 7026:                 state,
 7027:                 config,
 7028:                 self._repo(config.repo),
 7029:                 ledger=ledger,
 7030:                 replan_reason=json.dumps(normalized_reason, sort_keys=True),
 7031:                 planning_repo=(
 7032:                     Path(state["worktree"])
 7033:                     if isinstance(state.get("worktree"), str)
 7034:                     and Path(state["worktree"]).is_dir()
 7035:                     else None
 7036:                 ),

 7042:     def _reconcile_pending_commit(
 7043:         self,

 7697:         completed_offset = len(state.get("completed_commits", [])) * 1000
 7698:         existing_artifact_numbers = [
 7699:             int(match.group(1))
 7700:             for path in run_dir.iterdir()
 7701:             if (match := re.search(r"_(\d+)(?:\.[^.]+)$", path.name))
 7702:         ]
 7703:         self._artifact_offset = max([completed_offset, *existing_artifact_numbers], default=0) + 500
 7704:         task_plan = self._task_execution_plan(master_plan, task)
 7705:         ledger = MemoryLedger(
 7706:             run_dir,
 7707:             identifier,
 7708:             config.repo,
 7709:             state.get("objective_class", "capability_growth"),
 7710:         )
 7711:         tests = state.get("test_results")
 7712:         if not isinstance(tests, list):
 7713:             tests = []
 7714:         tests = list(tests)

 7716:         def block_resume(reason: str) -> None:
 7717:             self._save(
 7718:                 run_dir,
 7719:                 state,
 7720:                 "blocked",
 7721:                 block_reason=reason,
 7722:                 error_category="blocked",
 7723:                 error=reason,
 7724:                 test_results=tests,
 7725:             )
 7726:             raise FactoryError(reason, "blocked")

 7728:         def resume_repair(instruction: str, label: str) -> tuple[str, str, list[str], str]:
 7729:             previous_candidate: str | None = None
 7730:             previous_failure: dict[str, Any] | None = None
 7731:             repair_index = 0
 7732:             while True:
 7733:                 attempts = state.get("current_task_attempts", 0)
 7734:                 if not isinstance(attempts, int) or isinstance(attempts, bool) or attempts < 0:
 7735:                     block_resume("resumed task has an invalid attempt count")
 7736:                 if attempts >= config.max_task_attempts:
 7737:                     block_resume("resumed task attempt budget exhausted")
 7738:                 repair_index += 1
 7739:                 actual_label = label if repair_index == 1 else f"{label}_{repair_index}"
 7740:                 baseline = (
 7741:                     AgyBuilder._candidate_baseline_identity(worktree)
 7742:                     if isinstance(self.builder, AgyBuilder)
 7743:                     else None
 7744:                 )

 7746:                     run_dir,
 7747:                     state,
 7748:                     "building",
 7749:                     current_task_attempts=attempts + 1,

 7751:                     candidate_baseline_identity=baseline,
 7752:                     builder_dispatch={
 7753:                         "state": "reserved",
 7754:                         "label": actual_label,
 7755:                         "baseline_identity": baseline.get("complete_identity_sha256") if baseline else None,
 7756:                     },
 7757:                 )
 7758:                 self.builder.run(
 7759:                     worktree=worktree,
 7760:                     instruction=instruction,
 7761:                     allowed_files=task_plan["allowed_files"],
 7762:                     config=config,
 7763:                     run_dir=run_dir,
 7764:                     label=actual_label,
 7765:                 )
 7766:                 self._assert_source_unchanged(Path(config.repo), state, run_dir, f"after_{actual_label}")

 7768:                 self._save(
 7769:                     run_dir,
 7770:                     state,
 7771:                     "verifying",
 7772:                     active_attempt_ref=None,
 7773:                     builder_dispatch={"state": "completed", "label": actual_label},
 7774:                 )
 7775:                 next_sequence = max(
 7776:                     [item.get("sequence", 0) for item in tests if isinstance(item, dict)] + [0]
 7777:                 ) + 1

 7789:                     state,

 7909:                         task_id=task_id,
 7910:                         base_commit=base_commit,

 7914:                     block_resume(f"resumed verification could not produce a candidate: {exc}")

 7917:                     state,

 7927:             if not tests or not isinstance(tests[-1], dict) or not tests[-1].get("passed"):
 7928:                 block_resume("resumable review has no passing verification evidence")
 7929: 
 7930:         def load_orphan_review(
 7931:             digest: str, candidate_id: str, verification_contract: dict[str, Any]
 7932:         ) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]] | None:
 7933:             review_paths = sorted(
 7934:                 (
 7935:                     path
 7936:                     for path in run_dir.glob("sol_review_*.json")
 7937:                     if re.fullmatch(r"sol_review_[0-9]+\.json", path.name)
 7938:                 ),
 7939:                 key=lambda path: int(re.search(r"([0-9]+)", path.stem).group(1)),
 7940:                 reverse=True,
 7941:             )

 7943:                 try:
 7944:                     candidate_review = read_json(review_path)
 7945:                 except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeError) as exc:
 7946:                     raise FactoryError(
 7947:                         "resumable review artifact is unreadable", "blocked"
 7948:                     ) from exc

 7952:                     candidate_review.get("base_commit") != base_commit
 7953:                     or candidate_review.get("candidate_id") != candidate_id
 7954:                     or candidate_review.get("reviewed_diff_sha256") != digest
 7955:                 ):

 7958:                     validate_review(
 7959:                         candidate_review,
 7960:                         digest,
 7961:                         base_commit,
 7962:                         candidate_id,
 7963:                         self._task_review_criteria_ids(task_plan),
 7964:                         verification_contract,
 7965:                     )
 7966:                 except FactoryError as exc:
 7967:                     raise FactoryError(
 7968:                         "resumable review artifact does not validate for the current candidate",
 7969:                         "blocked",
 7970:                     ) from exc

 7974:         review_sequence = 1

 7992:                 run_dir,
 7993:                 tests,
 7994:                 task_id=task_id,
 7995:                 base_commit=base_commit,

 7997:             )

 7999:             source_context = self._candidate_source_context(
 8000:                 worktree,
 8001:                 task_plan["allowed_files"],
 8002:                 changed,
 8003:                 candidate_id,
 8004:                 run_dir,
 8005:                 review_sequence,
 8006:             )

 8011:                     config=config,
 8012:                     plan=task_plan,
 8013:                     diff_text=diff_text,

 8017:                     changed=changed,
 8018:                     candidate_source_context=source_context,
 8019:                     tests=tests,
 8020:                     sequence=review_sequence,
 8021:                 )
 8022:                 review_ref = f"sol_review_{artifact_sequence}.json"
 8023:             else:
 8024:                 review_ref, review = orphan_review
 8025:             if review.get("replan_reason") is not None:

 8027:                 self._save(run_dir, state, "reviewing", review_ref=review_ref)
 8028:                 reason = self._review_replan_reason(review_ref, review, state)
 8029:                 git(worktree, "reset", "--quiet")

 8031:             candidate_ref = state.get("candidate_ref")

 8033:                 r"candidate_[0-9]+\.json", candidate_ref

def probe(method):
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
        root=Path(directory); repo=make_repo(root)
        engine=FactoryEngine(state_root=root/'state', role_client=FakeRoles(), builder=FakeBuilder())
        config=Config(repo=str(repo), request='Make value return two', requested_files=['calc.py','test_calc.py'], test_command='python3 -m unittest -q', max_debug_attempts=2, max_review_cycles=2, test_timeout=30)
        identifier=engine.plan(config); engine.execute(identifier)
        state_path=root/'state'/identifier/'run_state.json'
        state=json.loads(state_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        result=state['test_results'][-1]
        result.update({'sequence':987654,'artifact_sequence':987654,'command':'attacker-command','returncode':99,'passed':False,'log':'attacker.log','log_sha256':'0'*64,'artifact_sha256':'1'*64})
        state_path.write_text(json.dumps(state),encoding='utf-8')
        try:
            observed=getattr(engine, method)(identifier)
            print(method,'TAMPER_ACCEPTED',observed['phase'])
        except Exception as exc:
            print(method,'TAMPER_REJECTED',type(exc).__name__,str(exc))
